# PetPrep — Project Handoff & State Directory

> Update after every work session (`/handoff`). Only claim what was verified in that session.
> Older session logs and status lines: `docs/engineering/handoff-archive.md` (incl. the 2026-09-16 "100 % complete" handoff, which was false).

## 1. Executive summary

- **2026-10-19: launch splash can no longer hang** (`fix/boot-hang-after-profile-switch`, PR #282, mobile JS only → OTA; fingerprint = main ios `0ca9398f…`, android `98911012…`): David's iPhone sat on »Loading…« ~14 min, no request reached the server. Cause: launch `GET /api/user` had no timeout (expo/fetch sets `timeoutInterval = 0` on iOS) and the `restoring` splash had no time limit / exit (`useLongOutage` never fires for a request that never settles). Fix: every restore step bounded (Keychain 3 s → `locked`, `/api/user` 8 s abort → `offline`, extras 2 s), restore + Keychain migration single-flight (a slow migration → `locked`, never a session or the login screen while the token key may be missing), `locked` on screen retries itself (0.5/1/2/4 s), 26 s watchdog (> `RESTORE_BUDGET_MS`) → offline screen, splash »Odjava« behind the parental gate, `logout()` with an unknown user keeps other children's shared sessions, late 401 after a timeout ignored. OTA ruled out. **Not verified on a phone.** Debt: why the connection stalled is unknown — check Caddy `Alt-Svc` (HTTP/3) vs compose publishing only TCP 443.
- **2026-10-19: Android payments key** (`chore/android-revenuecat-key`): public RevenueCat Android SDK key `EXPO_PUBLIC_REVENUECAT_ANDROID_KEY` in the `production` profile of `eas.json` (Android app added to the existing RevenueCat project by David; Play one-time product `petprep_challenge_12w`, purchase option `buy`, 49,99 €, EN + SL, active). `eas.json` is part of the fingerprint → ios `c57dec9f…`, android `0a3c14c4…`. Still open for David: RevenueCat Play service-account JSON, Android product in the current offering's package, Android OAuth client with the Play signing SHA-1.
- **2026-10-19: hotfix — cat routine ledger failed** (`fix/routine-type-varchar`): `pet_daily_routines.routine_type` was varchar(8) since M2-06, the cat routines `litter_scoop` (12) / `litter_change` (13) didn't fit → `RoutineLedgerService::closePet()` threw for every cat with a litter row (Postgres "value too long for type character varying(8)" at :03 / :33), the batch rolled back and the cat never closed a day (Care Score history / certificate frozen). Migration `2026_12_25_120000_widen_pet_daily_routines_routine_type` widens `routine_type` → varchar(32), `status` → varchar(16) (metadata-only in Postgres); regression test in `CatLitterScratchingGroomingTest` (fails without the migration). Backlog closes itself on the next ticks (`MAX_DAYS_PER_CLOSE` per tick). Also found: production Laravel logs live in the `app_storage` Docker volume (`docker exec backend-app-1 tail storage/logs/laravel.log`), NOT `/opt/petprep/repo/backend/storage/logs` (Sail-era leftover, last write 2026-10-01). Next: David's superadmin error-monitoring page.

Production log file lives in the `app_storage` volume (PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT §8).
- **2026-10-19: Health Connect privacy-policy link** (`feat/android-health-connect-privacy-rationale`, native → store build): the privacy link in the Health Connect permission dialog now opens the PetPrep privacy policy (new `HealthPermissionsRationaleActivity`, plugin `mobile/plugins/withHealthConnectPrivacyRationale.js`) instead of just opening the app — required for the Play health declaration. Verified with `expo prebuild` (manifest) + Jest; Kotlin not compiled here (no Android SDK) — the next EAS Android build is the check. **Both native fingerprints change** (plugin list is shared): ios `9c3754d7…`, android `55f78f55…` → existing iOS builds get no further OTA until a new iOS store build.
- **2026-10-19: first Android EAS build fixed** (`fix/M5-F10-android-fingerprint-gs-secret`): EAS refused it ("Runtime version calculated on local machine not equal …") because the build server keeps the `GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON` file env var outside `mobile/` under another name and the fingerprint hashed that file; `fingerprint.config.js` now ignores that path too. Verified: Android hash identical with no file / local file / EAS-style secret path (`bfafa82e…`); iOS unaffected. Next: David reruns `eas build -p android --profile production`.
- **2026-10-19: Android release lint fix** (`fix/android-locales-ios-only`): `mobile/locales/sl.json` iOS permission texts moved under `ios` — as top-level keys Expo also wrote them to Android `values-b+sl/strings.xml` and `lintVitalRelease` failed (ExtraTranslation). iOS InfoPlist output and both fingerprints unchanged; guard test `appLocales.test.ts`.
- **2026-10-17: child no longer signed out every ~2 days** (`fix/child-session-locked-keychain`, mobile JS only — OTA): David's child account landed on the login screen every ~48 h; force-quit + reopen restored it. Cause: token in SecureStore with default `WHEN_UNLOCKED`; iOS launches the app in the background while the phone is locked (step sync / pushes), `restoreSession` couldn't read the Keychain and returned `anonymous` → `reset()`. Fix: Keychain read error → `locked` (boot stays `restoring`, retried on the next foreground; 3 foreground locks in a row → login as before); session secrets (`petprep_auth_token`, child sessions) written with `AFTER_FIRST_UNLOCK`; one-time `migrateSessionKeychain` (delete + re-add — iOS never changes an existing item's access class) after a successful restore. Backend tokens never expire (`sanctum.expiration = null`) — no server change.
- **2026-10-15 (D20 disk hygiene):** server disk 30/38 GB was 23.4 GB Docker build cache (prune filter `until=168h` never matched). Deploy now caps cache at 2 GB (website 1 GB), bounded container logs 3 × 10 MB on all services, ≥ 70 % disk warning. David: one-time journald cap (PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT.md »Disk hygiene«); the logging change applies on the next production deploy (containers recreated).
- **2026-10-15 (perf, David: tasks too slow):** measured the Pest suite with JUnit — 82 % of its time was the per-test breed seeding (`seedLifeStageData()` before 1,641 tests: ~2,100 single-row inserts ≈ 0.9 s each). `BreedStageParamsSeeder` / `BreedConfigsSeeder::seedConfigs` now insert in multi-row `insertOrIgnore` chunks (same insert-only semantics): seeding 0.88 s → 0.12 s, full serial suite 34.6 → 13.7 min locally (3,267 tests green). Next (David's approval pending): breed pipeline schedule/shared-doc conflicts, agents run only affected tests (CI runs the full suite), disk cleanup on the server.

- **2026-10-14: M5-F13 the app survives a backend deploy** (`feat/M5-F13-maintenance-reconnect`, mobile JS only — OTA; native fingerprint unchanged: ios `9dcce1bc…`, android `cc53883a…`): `apiRequest` reports every answer to `serverStatusStore` (Zustand, UI state) — maintenance 503 *without* `reason`/`code` / 502 / 504 start the outage, any other answer ends it; a bare network failure never starts it (phone offline ≠ deploy) but keeps an existing one up. Queries: `shouldRetry` / `shouldRetryChildPet` retry deploy errors up to 6× with `Retry-After` (clamped 1–30 s) or 2 s … 30 s; 4xx handling unchanged (401 → logout handler exactly as before, 409 / 422 / 423 unchanged). `useServerRecovery` (AppNavigator) probes `GET /up` (3–30 s / `Retry-After`, foreground only) while the banner shows and `invalidateQueries({refetchType:'active'})` once the server is back. Banner `ServerUpdatingBanner` (i18n `common:serverUpdating`, dark in the HUD / light over all parent screens, `pointerEvents="none"`, fade off with Reduce Motion); HUD without state shows `hud-updating` instead of the load-error screen; parent dashboard / child report / timeline hide their error banners during the outage; launch during a deploy → `splash-updating` and the restore re-runs by itself on recovery. Child actions: new `ActionFailure` kind `updating` (text "PetPrep se ravno posodablja…") — **mutations are never auto-retried** (feed/water/clean/take_out have no idempotency key server-side; TanStack mutations default to 0 retries, `usePlay` keeps its offline-only ≤ 2 retries within the server's 10 s dedupe). Realtime: pusher-js reconnects by itself (verified in 8.6.0 source: closed → retry 1 s, `connected` → `subscribeAll`), but a failed channel auth is never retried by pusher-js — `withOutageRetry` retries `/api/broadcasting/auth` on deploy errors (20 attempts ≈ 5–8 min, through the existing `channelAuthGate`, stops on unmount); `usePetWebSocket` / `usePetChannels` call `onResubscribed` on every re-subscription → child `['child','pet']` / parent dashboard invalidated (missed broadcasts). Tests: 2614 Jest green (+57 new), `tsc` clean. **Not verified on a phone.** New debt: (1) `/up` is Laravel's health route — fine behind Caddy, but if Caddy itself is down the probe sees a network error and waits for the next real answer (no false recovery, just slower); (2) mutations other than the care actions (parent forms) show their own existing error text on a 503, not the "updating" one; (3) no server-side idempotency key for care actions — if ever wanted, add `Idempotency-Key` and then allow a retry. Next: David tests a deploy with the app open (child HUD + parent dashboard).
- **2026-10-14: D19 deploy without maintenance** (`feat/D19-deploy-without-maintenance`, PR #251, on top of D18): the new image runs `migrate:status --pending=3` against the live DB before maintenance (Laravel 11.56: 3 = pending, 0 = none — probed on a scratch DB); none → no `artisan down`, workers recreated before app, `migrate --force` kept as a no-op guard, one idempotent `artisan up`; pending / unknown → old flow. Caddy `lb_try_duration 10s` holds requests during the app swap. Reverb and Caddy are not restarted when the sha256 of their inputs + `.env` + base image id matches `releases/{reverb,caddy}.fingerprint` (first D19 deploy recreates and records). Harness 333/333. **Verify on the first real deploy:** the gap while `app` restarts and whether open WebSockets survive.

## 2. Decisions (2026-10-02)

- **Business model:** 12-week PetPrep Challenge **49.99 €** ~~with a 7-day free trial~~ — **no free trial since 2026-10-08 10:28 (David, M3-13): the free mutt is the try-out, the challenge starts with a purchase**; **Mutt stays free forever**. Proposed free/paid split in `docs/business/BUSINESS_MODEL.md` §7 (awaiting David's confirmation of the split).
- **Child login:** PIN only, no child email (M2-02) — backend + app built 2026-10-04 (`feat/M2-02-pin-only-child`).
- **Monorepo:** `pet-prep-mobile` merged into `pet-prep/mobile` — done.
- **Languages:** English (default) + Slovenian, more later (M1-18).
- **Package manager:** yarn 1 (root `package.json` declares it) — cleanup in M0-05.
- **Thresholds follow the displayed value** (David, 2026-10-03): warning/escalation thresholds, pet state and zero tracking (`*_zero_since` → phase 3, illness, game over) compare the rounded half-up value the child sees (`Pet::displayMetric`): 30.4 shows 30 → phase 1; 0.4 shows 0 → counts as zero. PRODUCT_SPEC §6.
- **Interim hygiene 1.5 %/h kept until M1-05** (David, 2026-10-03) — removed on `feat/M1-04-energy-hygiene-breeds` (random hygiene events replace it).

## 3. Known bugs & debt (top items — full list in AUDIT, DEPLOYMENT.md and the archive)

1. **TestFlight 1.24.4 still loops `POST /api/devices`** (iOS echoes the push token) — fixed on `main` (mobile) + own `throttle:devices` on the server, so the game is no longer blocked; the installed build still drains battery until a new build ships.
2. **Push registration edge case:** on Android, a real token rotation arriving as the first event inside the 10 s window is taken as the echo → registered only at next app start / login.
3. **No crash reporter:** render errors only reach the device console (`logRenderError`); Sentry needs David's OK (M5-05).
4. **Training:** schedule is sent to the app, so a modified app can fake praise taps — **accepted by David** (only system security matters); taps before an app restart are lost on resume; potty "asked to go out" only logged, no timeline row; minors m3/m4 (catch-up accidents use today's potty progress; decay applied after later gains) open in DECISIONS.
5. **Tests / tooling:** Pint fails repo-wide on 9 untouched files; two Pest runs in one checkout share `storage/framework/testing/disks` (use separate worktrees); cold-cache Jest timeout in `ChildHudScreen.behaviour.test.tsx`; GitHub Actions: `actions/checkout@v4` on deprecated Node 20, `ubuntu-latest` moves to Ubuntu 26 on 2026-10-19 — check CI after that date.
6. **i18n (M1-18) not verified on a device:** Hermes plural rules (polyfill `@formatjs/intl-pluralrules` loads only if needed), Android channel names after a switch, iOS Slovenian permission texts — all need the new native build. English copy (contract, push, legal/deletion texts, terms like "pup", "Dog school", "Mixed breed", "Place") awaits David's read-through. Terms / privacy URLs are the same for both languages.
7. **Security / hygiene still open:** test accounts in production DB (M0-15), EAS signing passwords in old repo history (M0-12), RevenueCat payments built but not live (D7a checklist: store products, webhook secret, SDK keys, then `PAYMENTS_ENFORCED=true`), PHP 8.3 prod vs 8.5 Sail (M0-11).
8. **About 40 decisions marked "čaka Davida"** in DECISIONS.md (incl. 7 behaviour questions from M5-R02: chewing 0.5, accidents vs. score, midnight clock, hard stop, quiet hours, walk for all breeds, adopted dogs).

## 4. Environment & configuration

- Local: Laravel Sail; `backend/.env` and `mobile/.env` exist (gitignored). `mobile/credentials.json` local only (gitignored).
- `FAL_AI_API_KEY` set locally (not on server yet). There is **no** fal webhook secret any more (ED25519 signature). Pending: RevenueCat, push, Sentry.
- Production queue worker must run for reference images (`queue` container exists).
- Production docs: `docs/PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT.md`, `docs/PRODUCTION_ENV.md`; findings: `docs/engineering/DEPLOYMENT.md`.
- SSH key of David's second Mac added to the server.
- **Cloud sessions (no Docker / Sail):** PostgreSQL 16 runs locally on `127.0.0.1:5432` (postgres / postgres). Run Pest with `DB_CONNECTION=pgsql DB_HOST=127.0.0.1 DB_DATABASE=testing_<branch> DB_USERNAME=postgres DB_PASSWORD=postgres php vendor/bin/pest --parallel`; in a worktree copy `vendor/` with `rsync -a --exclude=.git backend/vendor/ <worktree>/backend/vendor/` and delete it afterwards (disk is limited). The main checkout's `mobile/node_modules` is outdated (no `expo-notifications`) → `yarn install --frozen-lockfile` in the worktree.
- **Composer as root (cloud):** run `COMPOSER_ALLOW_SUPERUSER=1 composer install`, otherwise plugins are skipped, `vendor/pest-plugins.json` is missing and `pest --parallel` fails with "Unknown option". Start Postgres with `service postgresql start` and set the postgres password (`ALTER USER postgres PASSWORD 'postgres'`).
- **GitHub:** private repo on the free plan → no branch protection; the single gate is the `CI OK` check run. Actions logs can't be downloaded through the cloud proxy — read check-run **annotations** (the `plan` job prints its decision as a `::notice title=plan::`).

Built from day one: `DOG_GROOMING` 1 (RKC S298 "More than once a week" has no number → AKC S301 "Weekly brushing" → 1; PetMD S302 agrees; matted after 1 missed, M5-R13), `DOG_SOCIALISATION` with the PetMD quote (S302 "Early socialization in Brittany puppies is a must", M5-R17); no `MEAL_EXERCISE_GAP` row (no source). Sourced needs without a mechanic: nails trimmed once a month as needed (AKC S301 — the first M5-R19 source with a frequency) → existing **M5-R19**; ears checked regularly and cleaned as needed (AKC S301, PetMD S302) → existing **M5-R20** (both needs-david; the breed is added to the ROADMAP lines and the FEATURES rows). Suitability: suits active_family (RKC / AKC), family_pet (PetMD), children (PetMD "sweet and well-behaved with children of all ages", AKC "patient with children"); consider long_daily_exercise (RKC), needs_mental_stimulation (AKC), needs_company (PetMD), sheds (RKC), chases_small_pets (PetMD "hardwired with a high prey drive" — the breed is added to the prey-drive lists in PRODUCT_SPEC / FEATURES / PARENTS); no small_children, no other_pets, no frequent_grooming, no apartment / large_home (RKC "Small house"), not shorter_lifespan; no welfare chip (Breed Watch Category 1). Marketing: **in** (never promising health, never as a calm house dog, never a docked tail as a goal). AI appearance: RKC / FCI colours — orange and white (portrait default), liver and white, orange roan, liver roan, black and white, tricolour (AKC: only orange / liver and white, black-based colours weighted lowest); naturally short (bob) tail; never a solid colour, never white spots on the ears; `muzzle` trait; colour weights `verified=false`. Register: `brittany` exports as `coming_soon` until the next app release; health keys `hip_dysplasia_arthritis`, `epilepsy`, `hypothyroidism`, **new** `cataracts` (website needs the EN + SL words); breeder checklist `not_found` (the RKC breed page and the RKC Health Standard list no test). Tests: new `BrittanyBreedTest`; `GET /api/breeds` now 33 dogs; counts in the shared breed / grooming / socialisation / suitability / portrait tests (`BreedPortraitTest` 38 portraits × $0.15 = $5.70) and the mobile picker / catalogue / species tests; Pest 3,107 passed, Jest 2,539 passed, export tests 60 passed. **Open for David:** (1) senior 90 provisional until McMillan 2024 (Dogs Trust table S123) is checked — PetMD 12 y → 108 would be the alternative; (2) brushing 1× a week (AKC / PetMD) while the RKC says "More than once a week"; (3) other_pets not given (PetMD names "other pets" but also a high prey drive); (4) frequent_grooming not given (RKC vs AKC / PetMD); (5) M5-R19 nail trimming (AKC: once a month as needed) and M5-R20 ear care — whether, how often, who. (6) the Slovenian name »bretonski ptičar« (slug `bretonski-pticar`) was not checked against the KZS breed list — »bretonski španjel« is also in use (search keyword); confirm before links spread; (7) black and white / tricolour are standard colours in FCI / RKC but disqualifying in the AKC standard (S300) — kept with the lowest weights (FCI is the origin standard); remove them if David prefers. **Next:** review + CI on PR #239, merge and deploy; then the portrait (orange and white, natural bob tail) and the website page (»Kmalu« until the next app release). **Update 2026-10-12 (M5-R09b »Ali si vedel?« breed facts — server + admin, branch `feat/M5-R09b-breed-facts-backend`, PR open, not merged; spec `docs/product/DID_YOU_KNOW_SPEC.md`):** migration `2026_12_04_120000_create_breed_facts` (additive, reversible): `breed_facts` (key unique, breed = BreedType value without enum CHECK but `NOT IN (mutt, domestic_cat)`, category / audience / status CHECKs, family + health ⇒ parent, approved ⇔ approved_at, content_hash), `breed_fact_reads` (unique user + fact), `breed_fact_shows` (unique child + pet + local date, program_week). `BreedFactService` (who sees what, reads, fact of the day, `autoShowRefusal` order no_pet › non_pedigree › locked › no_image › no_fact › first_day › quiet_hours › shown_today › week_cap › too_soon), `BreedFactPayload` (Scramble types), `BreedFactController`, `BreedFactPolicy`, `BreedFactsRequest`; routes `GET /api/breeds/{breed}/facts` + `POST /api/breed-facts/{fact}/read` (`ability:parent,child`), `GET /api/child/fact-of-the-day` + `POST /api/child/fact-of-the-day/shown` (child group). No ClientFeature (additive endpoints). **Content:** canonical `docs/research/breed-facts/<breed>.json` (format: its README), shipped byte-for-byte copy `backend/database/data/breed-facts/` (the production image is `backend/` only) — `BreedFactImportTest` fails when they differ, `scripts/ci-plan.sh` runs backend tests for `docs/research/breed-facts/**`; `php artisan breeds:import-facts {--breed=*} {--dry-run} {--print-hashes} {--dir=}` (manual; a file with any error or unknown key is rejected whole; `approved` in a file refused; `verified` only with a matching `verified_hash`, else draft; unchanged content keeps its status / approval; changed content → verified-with-proof or draft, approval cleared). **Admin:** `/admin/breed-facts` (EN + SL) — filters, verify / approve (superadmin) / back to draft, bulk approve of verified rows, view page with child + parent card preview. Export: `breed_facts.{reads, auto_shows}`; game-data reset keeps `breed_facts`, wipes reads / shows. `mobile/src/api/schema.ts` regenerated (additive; spec exported with `scramble:export` against a local Postgres and served to `scripts/generate-api-types.mjs`). **Next:** R09a content agent writes `docs/research/breed-facts/border_collie.json` / `maine_coon.json` → copy to backend → deploy → `breeds:import-facts --dry-run`, then import → David approves in the admin; then R09c (child sheet, chip, Moja pasma — calm moment in the app) and R09d (parent card + guide). Open product questions in DECISIONS (»Odprta vprašanja«, 2026-10-12 M5-R09b).

**Update 2026-10-12 (M5-R10-26 Boxer / boksar, branch `feat/M5-R10-26-boxer`, PR #209 + portrait PR #211 + website PR #48):** twenty-second paid dog, the eighth breed appended after the 20 (AKC 2024 No. 17 "Boxer", S174; queue row 26). Dog touch points as the Dobermann: migration `2026_12_02_120000` widens only `pets_breed_type_check`; `DOG_BREEDS` + `boxerProfile()` (`CONFIRMED_R10_BOXER`): stages 9 / 36 / **102** (0.75 × McMillan 2024 median 11.3 y via the Dogs Trust table, S123 — not provisional; alternatives 94 VetCompass 2023 median 10.46 y S220, 84 Dogs Trust > 7 y S14), arrival 2 / 9 / 36 / 102, adult 120 min (RKC S215 "More than 2 hours per day"; PDSA S219 "a minimum of two hours exercise every day") → 12,000 steps, puppy 10 min × age capped at 120 (12 months), senior 90 → 9,000, learning × 1.0 (Coren 48, Average, S35), FCI 144 (S213 French standard 2008; nomenclature S214) Group 2 Section 2.1 Mastiff type, Germany, RKC standard weight 25–32 kg (males about 30–32, females about 25–27, S217), height 57–63 / 53–59 cm, growth end 15–18 months (S10 Large), RKC size Large, sort 220; Border Collie care rates. Built mechanics from day one: `DOG_GROOMING` 1/week (RKC "Grooming: Once a week", PDSA weekly brush; picker hint »Del 12-tedenskega izziva · česanje 1× na teden«), `MEAL_EXERCISE_GAP` 60 min (PDSA "Remember to leave a gap between eating and exercising.") and `DOG_SOCIALISATION` with the PDSA quote. Suitability: suits active_family, family_pet, large_home; consider long_daily_exercise, **new key `short_muzzle_breathing`** (EN "shorter muzzle — can have breathing problems", SL »krajši gobček — lahko težje diha«; welfare rule, a new key instead of the flat-face / heat chip brachycephalic_breathing — no Boxer source names heat: VetCompass S220 "moderately brachycephalic", RKC Breed Watch Category 2 "Pinched nostrils" / "Excessively short muzzle" S216, PDSA BOAS), not_for_small_children, experienced_owner, **new key `needs_company`** (EN "loves company — best with someone home most of the day", SL »rad ima družbo — naj bo nekdo večino dneva doma«; PDSA separation anxiety — also in the `BreedCatalogResource` union and `schema.ts`, additive), chews_when_bored, sheds, chases_small_pets; never children / small_children / hypoallergenic. Marketing: **excluded** by the runbook rule (moderately brachycephalic, BVA S92) — like the French Bulldog and the Bulldog; in the app yes. AI appearance: fawn with a black mask (portrait default) or brindle, white never more than a third, moderate muzzle with wide open nostrils, natural uncropped ears and natural undocked tail (the AKC's cropped ears / docked tail never drawn). Register: health keys **new** `short_face_breathing`, **new** `corneal_ulcers`, **new** `skin_folds_allergies`, **new** `heart_cardiomyopathy_aortic_stenosis`, hip_dysplasia_arthritis, epilepsy, certain_cancers — no numbers; breeder checklist RKC Best Practice elbow, eye and hip schemes (no minimum list). The new health keys need EN + SL words on the website (website PR follows). **Open for David:** (1) Boxer marketing exclusion — only moderately short-faced, he may want it in marketing as he decided for the Cavalier; (2) face folds kept clean and dry (PDSA S219) → M5-R14 (flat-faced dog care) now also lists the Boxer; (3) SL name »boksar« (common name, KZS page not opened). Portrait + website follow in their own PRs. Not verified on a device.

**Update 2026-10-11 (M5-R10-18 Bengal / bengalska mačka, branch `feat/M5-R10-18-bengal`, PR #176):** sixth paid cat, fifth cat on the unattended runbook. Touch points as the Norwegian Forest Cat: migration `2026_11_20_120000` widens `pets_breed_type_check` and the cat list of `pets_species_breed_check`; `CAT_BREEDS` gains `Bengal` (general rows + kitten arrival 3 `CONFIRMED_R10_BENGAL` + grooming 1×/week `CONFIRMED_R10_BENGAL_GROOMING` + lifespan 8.51 from the C14 VetCompass row, decision `CONFIRMED_R10_BENGAL`); `GROOMING_CAT_BREEDS` (mobile) and the export's grooming rule follow. New: the register weight is **one number** (PDSA average 5.5 kg, qualifier `about` — no A/B source split by sex), growth `up_to` 24 months (VCA C46). Appearance from WCF BEN 2024-08-01 (C42, quotes) + FIFe BEN 01.01.2026 (C43, tool summary only) + FIFe colours via World Cat Congress (C47): brown / silver / snow, spotted (rosettes) or marbled, spotted belly; never white patches, vertical stripes or ear tufts; blue eyes only with snow; feature "a domestic cat, not a wild cat"; portrait default brown rosetted. Register health keys `pra_pk_def` (new website words), `heart_hcm`, `kneecap_luxation`, `cancer_risk`; breeder checklist `listed` — `dna_pra_b` (new website word) + `dna_pk_def` minimum (GCCF Active Register, K22). **Open for David:** (1) **marketing** — the Bengal descends from the Asian leopard cat; FelineVMA (C48) opposes wild × domestic breeding and discourages F1–F3 ownership, The Cat Group incl. BSAVA (C50) does not support breeding / keeping wild cat hybrids; neither advises against advertising registered domestic Bengals, so the runbook rule keeps it in marketing ("only as a registered domestic breed, never wild, never easy for small children") — decide whether to use it at all; (2) PetMD: "may not be the best fit for families with small children" is register text only (cats have no suitability tags yet); (3) colour weights unsourced; (4) PDSA / PetMD say the Bengal needs plenty of stimulation and exercise — built as the general cat play / scratching rules (no sourced number for more play); a higher play goal for very active cat breeds would be a new rule; (5) website words for the new keys `pra_pk_def` and `dna_pra_b` come with the website PR (the registry build fails without them); (6) the C14 value 8.51 y is the 2026-10-08 capture (BBC Science Focus confirms 8.5 y; PDSA says 12–16 y — a different measure).

**Update 2026-10-11 (cat grooming for every sourced breed, branch `feat/M5-R10-cat-grooming`, PR #169):** David's standing rule — every sourced breed need is built in the app, never left as "proposal, not built" (DECISIONS, runbook §3 + §6 item 10). British Shorthair grooming 1× per week (VCA C28), Ragdoll 2× per week (upper bound of PetMD C32), matted coat when at least half of the week's slots are missed (`CatChoreService::mattedAfterMissed()` replaces `MATTED_AFTER_MISSED`; Maine Coon still 2 of 3). New `breed_stage_params` rows are insert-only seeded on deploy (cats hidden, no live pets). Mobile: `hasGroomingRoutine()` drives the brushing contract line and the parent report row; picker hints name the brushing. Register: `grooming_rule` for both breeds (the website shows it on the next export). The branch name holds the M5-R10 lock while open.

**Update 2026-10-11 (M5-R11-06b, international checklist + genetics as »in development« on the site):** `buyer_checklist` items may carry `country` (ISO alpha-2); the website shows universal items to everyone and a country's rules only on its language pages (`checklistCountry` in `src/content/registry/{en,sl}.ts`: sl → SI; en → none + a "laws differ" note) and drops sources cited only by hidden national items. A new country = items with its `country` + a locale whose `checklistCountry.code` matches. Genetics added as planned on home / AI assistant / investors (no own-test, lab or price details).

**Update 2026-10-10 (M5-R10-13 Siberian Husky / sibirski haski, branch `feat/M5-R10-13-siberian-husky`, PR #161; portrait PR #162, website PR #27):** fourteenth paid dog breed, tenth unattended run on `docs/engineering/ADD_BREED_RUNBOOK.md` §3 — every game value is a standing rule (`proposed_game_parameters.siberian_husky.*.decision`; seeder constant `CONFIRMED_R10_SIBERIAN_HUSKY`). Edge cases: lifespan — McMillan 2024 names no Siberian Husky in its text (S135 / S54), its Supplementary Table 3 and the Dogs Trust breed table (S123) were not reachable, Teng 2022's Husky life table was not reachable, no VetCompass median → the RKC lower bound "Over 10 years" (S160, PDSA S162 the same) → senior 90 months, **provisional** (alternatives: 88 from the Finnish average 9.75 y, IPFD S165 — not a UK median; 84 from Dogs Trust > 7 y); **new consider key `secure_fencing`** (runbook new-tag rule: PDSA S162 "Owners need a very secure garden with high fences …"); no family_pet / children / small_children (PDSA: adult-only or teenage households, not with smaller children), no other_pets (prey drive), no often_alone (separation anxiety); size label RKC "Medium-Large" (PDSA says "Large") while growth end uses the PetMD Medium class (12–15 months, FCI 15.5–28 kg); RKC Breed Watch "Category 2" lists only "Too fat" / "Too thin" (show body condition) → not a conformation welfare breed, may be used in marketing (never shown as an easy dog for small children); register export got FCI group 5 (Spitz and primitive types, section 1 Nordic Sledge Dogs) and a new size value `medium_large` (website words needed), origin US (exists), health keys `hip_dysplasia`, `inherited_eye_disease`, `epilepsy` (all exist). Portrait + register (2026-10-11): the first portrait dispatch (run 38090284398) was refused — AI Lab daily budget ($3.00 of $3.00, UTC day) used up; the run kept the queue lock with draft PR #162 and retried after the UTC reset: run 38097387532 (grey and white with a white mask, blue eyes, verdict OK — realistic photo, one adult dog, plain fog-grey backdrop, erect ears, brush tail, natural proportions) — only the Husky webp + manifest entry taken (the run regenerated the Labrador again; main's Labrador kept), portrait PR #162, website PR #27 (new FCI group 5 words, size `medium_large` words + `SIZE_ORDER` in CatalogueExplorer / views). **Open for David:** verify the McMillan 2024 Siberian Husky median (senior 90 is provisional; replace via Filament — the seeder is insert-only); the PDSA quotes (S162) were capped at ~120 characters by the fetch tool — spot-check them; confirm the `secure_fencing` wording.

**Update 2026-10-10 (M5-R10-12 Bernese Mountain Dog / bernski planšarski pes, branch `feat/M5-R10-12-bernese-mountain-dog`, PR #159; portrait PR #160, website PR #26):** thirteenth paid dog breed, ninth unattended run on `docs/engineering/ADD_BREED_RUNBOOK.md` §3 — every game value is a standing rule (`proposed_game_parameters.bernese_mountain_dog.*.decision`; seeder constant `CONFIRMED_R10_BERNESE_MOUNTAIN_DOG`). New edge cases: **no rule row fitted the senior boundary** — McMillan 2024 has the Bernese only in its supplementary table (text S135 / correction S54 do not name it; Dogs Trust PDFs failed), there is no VetCompass Bernese paper and the RKC band is "Under 10 years" (no lower bound) → the closest row: the peer-reviewed Swiss breed median 8.4 y (Klopfenstein 2016, S156) → senior 76 months as **Claude's suggestion, not confirmed**: the senior `starts_at_months` / `arrival_age_months` rows are seeded `verified => false` with decision `PROPOSED_R10_BERNESE_SENIOR` (new optional profile key `arrival_meta_overrides`), so `data_verified` is false for a senior Bernese; the seeder is insert-only, so David's later change goes through Filament (alternatives: 91 from McMillan 10.1 y via Wikipedia, tier C; 84; 63). Runbook §3 now says how to handle this case; **new consider key `shorter_lifespan`** (runbook new-tag rule: RKC / PDSA / Woodgreen all under 10 years); conflicting child statements (PDSA vs Woodgreen) → neither child tag; growth end uses the PetMD Giant class (18–24 months) while the size label stays RKC "large"; register export got a new qualifier `less_than`, origin CH and FCI group 2 (website words needed); the Swiss median and degenerative myelopathy are not exported (review findings S3 / S4). The breed name contains "Dog" / "pes" → `speciesCoverage` DOG_ONLY reason. Open for David: confirm the 76-month senior boundary (or replace it once McMillan 2024 Supplementary Table 3 is read) and the `shorter_lifespan` wording.

### 2026-10-10 (cloud, mobile-engineer) — M5-R06-09 part A: cats on in the app (branch `feat/M5-R06-09a-cat-ui-ready`)
- **Switch:** `CAT_UI_READY = true` (David 2026-10-10 08:35). Tests now cover both server states: cats on (species step, `species_cat` in catalogue / generate-pin / pin-login) and cats off (dog-only catalogue → exactly the old dog flow, no fallback, no error); a build without the cat UI still drops cats (`showableSpecies(false)`).
- **Paywall / paid-challenge texts per species (EN + SL):** new `tPets(key, group)` (`@/i18n`) + `petGroup(pets)` (`modules/species`): no cat → dog text byte-identical; only cats → `cat:override.*` ("muca", parents formal, EN "the cat"); dogs + cats → neutral `cat:mixed.*` ("ljubljenček" / "pet"). Per-pet lines (`unborn`, `paused`, `refused`, buy-button a11y) follow the pet's own species. Wired into `ChallengeScreen`, `ChallengeBanner`, `PurchasesRow` (`challengePets()` helper in `purchaseEntry.ts`), `ChallengeBuyButton` (`species` prop), `DeletionConfirmForm` (`paidPetGroup`), delete errors in `AccountCard` / `FamilyChildrenCard` (`paidDeletionGroup()` in `account.ts`: the removed pets whose purchase is lost, else all removed pets).
- **T8:** the four payment `DOG_ONLY` entries are gone (33 → 29); new block requires a cat variant for every payment text and a mixed variant for every group text; placeholder / plural checks cover `cat:mixed.*`.
- **Debt:** (1) cat / mixed payment texts are Claude drafts — David reads them on the device (SL "Brezplačna domača mačka je brezplačna za vedno", "pravo mačko"); (2) the 08d note stands: a cat child's Android channel may be saved as "Opomniki za kužo" if push registration runs before the pet is known — check on the device.

### 2026-10-08 late night (cloud, backend-engineer) — M5-R06-05 cat litter, scratching, grooming (PR #100 merged + deployed 2026-10-09)
- **David decided ~22:20 (final, (D) removed):** scratching resolve deadline = 2 h outside quiet hours exactly like dog chewing, then the existing ladder; matted coat = weekly counter (≥ 2 of the program week's 3 groomings missed, at the weekly birthday); matted has no penalty (visible, next grooming ~2× longer resolves it, only the missed routines count). Overdue weekly change → 2 h scoop deadline until changed (confirmed).
- **Built (backend only, cats hidden):** migration `2026_10_27_120000_add_cat_litter_scratching_grooming` — `pet_hygiene_events.kind` + `litter_use` (not a mess) / `litter_accident` / `scratching` (one per day, partial unique), `due_at` + `escalated_at`; `pets.coat_matted_at` + `grooming_weeks_checked`; `pet_care_sessions.kind` + `scratching`; CHECKs for 7 activity types, 3 routine types, 2 event kinds, push `litter_reminder`. `HygieneEventService` schedules `litter_uses_per_day` uses (own RNG stream, `hygiene_scheduled_through` pointer) and applies them without touching hygiene; `openEvents()` and the vet's recovery see messes only. `LitterService` (deadline fixed at the use: 4 h outside quiet hours, 2 h while the previous program week's `litter_change` routine was missed and nothing changed since; `escalateDue` writes the `litter_accident` at the deadline — not after a freeze / outage; scoop). `CatChoreService` (server-led stroke mini-games for the weekly litter change and Maine Coon grooming, `config/cat_care.php`; grooming ≤ 1 per local day, ≤ 3 per program week, not in quiet hours; `closeGroomingWeek` → matted coat; `expireStale` for all three new kinds → tick broadcast `care_session_ended`). `ScratchingService` (decided once a day after the midnight close from `play_missed_on`, reusing `behaviour_scheduled_through` as the cat pointer; start = carry, finish = `praise_ms`, success iff landing + 150 ms ≤ praise ≤ landing + 3 s). `LifeStageService::programPeriodAt` (program weeks on the birth's wall clock, payment-lock aware, DST-safe). Ledger: `litter_scoop` per use, the cat's messes as `clean` (`litter_accident` / `scratching`), weekly `litter_change` + `grooming` slots on the day the program week ends (excused at ≥ 50 % freeze); lookback 9 days only when cats are in the batch. ### 2026-10-08 evening (cloud, orchestrator) — deploy of #91 failed: backup retention vs. pre-reset/
- PR #91 (M5-R06-01) merged; the deploy failed twice. Log from David: backup created, then `find: '/opt/petprep/backups/pre-reset': Permission denied` → `pre-deployment backup failed` → maintenance left, nothing changed (production stayed on 0df3aa5). Cause: `reset-game-data.sh` (run by David) writes `backups/pre-reset/` as root with umask 077; `backup-production-db.sh` retention `find` recursed into it. Fix PR `fix/backup-retention-maxdepth`: `-maxdepth 1`, retention failure only warns, paths overridable for the new harness (`scripts/tests/backup-production-db.test.sh`, 7 checks, in CI + shellcheck). Merged as #92 — the deploy still failed, because `deploy-production.sh` runs the **installed** `/opt/petprep/scripts/backup-production-db.sh` (refreshed only after the code switch). Follow-up PR `fix/deploy-uses-release-backup-script`: the deploy runs the backup script from the release being deployed, falling back to the installed copy (deploy harness 200 checks incl. 2 new cases). Merging it deploys #91 + #92.
